- Supplemental code locations (2026-09-02 Feature `6.3ae`/`8.4` overlapping explicit flushes): `apps/editor/public/adapters/storage/autosave.js:13-14`, `103-106`, `290-324`, and `378-392` track a pending explicit flush while a dirty project-file save is suppressed or busy, clear that transient flag with autosave state, and immediately flush accumulated edits after the current verified save succeeds; `test/project-file-storage-adapters.test.mjs:239-295` holds the first save in flight while two later World Spine-style dirty mutations request flushes and verifies that one immediate follow-up write contains all placements; `test/project-service-storage.test.mjs:445-542` performs three sequential metadata-only location saves without reload, then reconstructs the persisted World Spine model and verifies Earth/Earth/Mars row identity in both project index and scene store. Workflow: each World Spine row assignment updates the canonical project record and requests a project-file flush -> an in-flight write snapshots its current state while later row mutations remain dirty -> busy flush calls mark one immediate follow-up as pending -> completion writes the latest accumulated snapshot through the same autosave boundary before returning. Persistence/rendering implications: the flag is transient controller state, no project schema changes, and only the active project-file destination is written; browser reload sees every saved row assignment without eager scene-body hydration. - Feature 8.4f - Autosave out-of-sync diagnostics: `apps/editor/public/adapters/storage/autosave.js:23-39` and `apps/editor/public/adapters/storage/autosave.js:233-267` normalize and store the originating failure cause on `projectFileAutosaveBlocked`, `apps/editor/public/adapters/storage/project-persistence-service.js:2174-2182`, `apps/editor/public/adapters/storage/project-persistence-service.js:2224-2228`, and `apps/editor/public/adapters/storage/project-persistence-service.js:2329-2332` pass browser-handle, desktop-path, and Save As fallback errors through the persistence boundary, `apps/editor/public/shared/project-autosave-status.js:58-120` renders the cause in the Autosave `Out of sync` note, `test/project-file-storage-adapters.test.mjs:319-345` covers the status model text, and `test/project-persistence-service.test.mjs:255-285` verifies stale-readback causes survive on the blocked state. Workflow: a file write or readback verification fails after the app built a canonical project DTO snapshot -> the latest snapshot is preserved in browser cache -> autosave blocks retries for that target with `reason: "write-failed"` plus the adapter error message -> the header tells the author why the project file is out of sync and keeps Ctrl+S as the explicit retry path. Persistence/rendering implications: no DTO mutation path bypasses `ProjectPersistenceService`; this only improves the author-facing status and log context around failed project-file sync. Verification: focused storage/persistence tests plus syntax checks.
- Feature 8.4e - Metadata-only scene DTO body preservation: `apps/editor/public/adapters/storage/project-persistence-service.js:394-589`, `apps/editor/public/adapters/storage/project-persistence-service.js:1049-1068`, and `apps/editor/public/adapters/storage/project-persistence-service.js:1093-1188` reconstruct recoverable scene bodies, merge metadata-only retained scene-store records before project-file snapshot export, and read the current project file to recover body chunks when an already-open session has a collapsed in-memory scene store, `apps/editor/public/adapters/storage/project-repository.js:177-806` preserves existing scene chunk bodies even when `changedSceneIds` marks a metadata-only scene DTO as changed, and `apps/editor/public/features/world-spine/world-spine-location-row-service.js:205-251` prevents row-location scene-store upserts from replacing retained body fields. Workflow: a World Spine row rename/drop changes scene/event location DTOs, the project persistence boundary records the mutation, the browser scene-cache writer and project-file snapshot builder merge the new metadata onto existing, line-backed, or current-file recovered `editorText`/`blocks`, and autosave can proceed to verified writeback without tripping the collapsed-body guard. Verification: `test/world-spine-panel.test.mjs:313-344`, `test/project-service-storage.test.mjs:365-378`, `test/project-persistence-service.test.mjs:1008-1081`, focused persistence/storage/panel tests, and full `npm test`.
- Feature 8.5 - Project metrics and record derivation: `apps/editor/public/adapters/storage/project-index.js`, `apps/editor/public/state/project-record-state.js:11-246`, `test/project-record-state.test.mjs`, `test/project-service-storage.test.mjs`.
- Feature 8.6 - Scrivener project port: `apps/editor/public/editor-model.js:22-34`, `apps/editor/public/adapters/storage/scrivener-import-service.js:4-6`, `apps/editor/public/adapters/storage/scrivener-import-service.js:599-641`, `apps/editor/public/adapters/storage/scrivener-import-service.js:887-916`, `apps/editor/public/adapters/storage/scrivener-import-service.js:940-1160`, `apps/editor/public/adapters/storage/scrivener-import-service.js:1182-1532`, `apps/editor/public/adapters/storage/scrivener-import-service.js:1794-2043`, `apps/editor/public/adapters/storage/project-persistence-service.js:940-948`, `apps/editor/public/adapters/storage/project-persistence-service.js:1473-1554`, `apps/editor/public/adapters/storage/project-persistence-service.js:1793-1887`, `apps/editor/public/shell/editor-chrome.js:292-307`, `apps/editor/public/app.js:1242`, `apps/editor/public/app.js:1858-1862`, `apps/editor/public/app.js:2666-2668`, `apps/editor/public/app.js:11729-11730`, `apps/editor/public/app.js:13126-13129`, `test/scrivener-import-service.test.mjs:15-20`, `test/scrivener-import-service.test.mjs:65-161`, `test/project-persistence-service.test.mjs:775-805`, `test/editor-chrome.test.mjs:200-221`. Workflow: the author ports a Scrivener package from the File menu, Draft/Manuscript documents become scene drafts, inline comments and footnotes become a generated Metadata Console group, recognized Research/WorldBuilding reference documents become World Spine catalogue entities, the original `.scriv` package remains read-only source provenance, and Save As immediately asks for the ABE `.abe-project.json` destination. Execution flow: `chooseScrivenerProjectForImport` collects the package files -> `buildScrivenerProjectSnapshotFromFiles` parses the binder, imports manuscript text and metadata, creates `scrivener-reference` entities with extracted fields and source paths, writes import counts, and activates the resulting ABE project through the normal persistence boundary -> `saveProjectSnapshotAs` opens the file-name prompt with the imported title, writes the selected project file or download fallback, stores the destination in project settings, and reports saved/cancelled/fallback state. Verification: focused Scrivener import tests cover RTF fidelity, comments/footnotes, editor preferences, reference character/ship/station promotion, category IDs, extracted fields, source archive provenance, and manuscript-line exclusion; project persistence/editor chrome tests cover the File-menu route and immediate ABE save prompt, and the Serva Vitae dry import verifies `John's Dream` as the first scene with 56 comments, 1 footnote, and 57 resolved anchors.
